    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c01",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "In the mythology of the Andean civilizations of South America, the amaru or katari (Aymara) is a mythical serpent or dragon.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi peradaban Andes di Amerika Selatan, amaru atau katari (Aymara) adalah ular atau naga mitos.",
        "en": "In the mythology of the Andean civilizations of South America, the amaru or katari (Aymara) is a mythical serpent or dragon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c02",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "In Inca mythology, Amaru is a huge double-headed serpent that dwells underground, at the bottom of lakes and rivers.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Inka, Amaru adalah ular raksasa berkepala dua yang tinggal di bawah tanah, di dasar danau dan sungai.",
        "en": "In Inca mythology Amaru is a huge two-headed serpent living underground, at the bottom of lakes and rivers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c03",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "It represents infinity, knowledge, and cosmic renewal, and connects the earthly world with the spiritual.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amaru melambangkan ketakterbatasan, pengetahuan, dan pembaruan kosmis, serta menghubungkan dunia fana dengan dunia roh.",
        "en": "It stands for infinity, knowledge and cosmic renewal and links the earthly world with the spiritual."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c04",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Illustrated with the heads of a bird and a puma, Amaru can be seen emerging from a central element in the center of a stepped mountain or pyramid motif in the Gateway of the Sun at Tiwanaku, Bolivia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amaru berkepala burung dan puma tampak pada motif gunung berundak di Gerbang Matahari Tiwanaku, Bolivia.",
        "en": "Amaru with the heads of a bird and a puma appears on a stepped-mountain motif on the Gateway of the Sun at Tiwanaku, Bolivia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c05",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "When illustrated on religious vessels, amaru is often seen with bird-like feet and wings, so that it resembles a dragon.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada bejana ritual, amaru sering digambarkan berkaki dan bersayap seperti burung sehingga mirip naga.",
        "en": "On religious vessels amaru is often shown with bird-like feet and wings, so it resembles a dragon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c06",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Amaru was also associated with the vital waters that irrigated the farmlands and upon which the Aymara people depended.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amaru juga dikaitkan dengan air yang mengairi ladang dan menjadi tumpuan hidup orang Aymara.",
        "en": "Amaru was also linked with the waters that irrigated the fields on which the Aymara depended."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c07",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "In addition, Amaru was associated with the underworld, as well as the earth and earthquakes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amaru juga dikaitkan dengan dunia bawah, bumi, dan gempa bumi.",
        "en": "Amaru was also linked with the underworld, the earth and earthquakes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c08",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Despite their usual benevolent portrayal, some Amarus display a violent side.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski biasanya digambarkan baik, sebagian amaru menunjukkan sisi kejam.",
        "en": "Though usually portrayed as benevolent, some amarus show a violent side."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c09",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "According to Professor Brian S. Bauer, (Sacred serpent) was a serpent or dragon deity often depicted as a winged serpent, with crystalline eyes, a reddish snout, a llama head, taruka horns, and a fish tail",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Profesor Brian S. Bauer, amaru sering digambarkan sebagai ular bersayap bermata kristal, bermoncong kemerahan, berkepala llama, bertanduk rusa taruka, dan berekor ikan.",
        "en": "According to Professor Brian S. Bauer, amaru was often depicted as a winged serpent with crystalline eyes, a reddish snout, a llama head, taruka horns and a fish tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amaru-mythology-c10",
      "source_id": "amaru-mythology-s2",
      "quote": "Les deux têtes représentent religieusement les ténèbres et la lumière",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kedua kepalanya secara religius melambangkan kegelapan dan terang.",
        "en": "Its two heads religiously represent darkness and light."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Boitatá adalah ular api dalam cerita rakyat pribumi Brasil yang menjaga ladang dan hutan dari pembakaran; namanya juga dipakai untuk api gaib dan banteng bernapas api.",
    "en": "Boitatá is a fire snake of Brazilian native folklore that guards fields and forests against fire; the name is also used for a will-o'-the-wisp and a fire-breathing bull.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "boitata-c01",
      "boitata-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat pribumi Brasil, Boitatá bisa berarti api gaib, ular api yang menjaga ladang dan hutan dari pembakaran, atau banteng mitos yang menyemburkan api dari hidungnya. Ia juga dikenal di Paraguay, Uruguay, dan Argentina, dan namanya dipakai sebagai momok untuk menakut-nakuti anak.",
      "en": "In Brazilian native folklore Boitatá can mean a will-o'-the-wisp, a fire snake guarding fields and forests from burning, or a mythical bull that shoots fire from its nostrils. It is also known in Paraguay, Uruguay and Argentina, and the name serves as a bogeyman to frighten children.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "boitata-c01",
        "boitata-c03",
        "boitata-c04",
        "boitata-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut Magalhães (1876), ular api ini tunduk pada dewi bulan Jaci, pelindung tumbuhan; kadang ia menjelma kayu menyala dan membakar pelaku pembakaran sampai mati. Satu sumber menyebutnya ular api raksasa yang menyerang perusak hutan dan satwa Amazon.",
      "en": "According to Magalhães (1876) the fire snake serves the moon goddess Jaci, protector of plants; sometimes it turns into a burning log and burns arsonists to death. One source calls it a huge fire serpent that attacks those who harm the forests and animals of the Amazon.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "boitata-c07",
        "boitata-c08",
        "boitata-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Catatan tertua berasal dari surat José de Anchieta bertanggal 31 Mei 1560, yang menyebut baetatá, \"benda api\", sebagai berkas cahaya yang menyerang dan membakar orang di dekat laut, sungai, dan pantai. Di beberapa daerah ia disebut Jean de la foice atau João Galafuz.",
      "en": "The oldest record is José de Anchieta's letter of 31 May 1560, which calls the baetatá, \"thing of fire\", a beam of light that attacks and burns people near the sea, rivers and beaches. In some areas it is called Jean de la foice or João Galafuz.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "boitata-c02",
        "boitata-c05",
        "boitata-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "boitata-c01",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "Boitatá (from Tupi language), in Brazilian native folklore, refers to either a will-o'-the-wisp, a mythical fire snake which guards against humans setting fire to the fields or forests, or a bull-like creature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat pribumi Brasil, Boitatá merujuk pada api gaib (will-o'-the-wisp), ular api mitos yang menjaga ladang dan hutan dari pembakaran oleh manusia, atau makhluk mirip banteng.",
        "en": "In Brazilian native folklore, Boitatá refers to a will-o'-the-wisp, a mythical fire snake that guards fields and forests against people setting fires, or a bull-like creature."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c02",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "It is also called Jean de la foice or Jean Delafosse (\"John of the sickle\") in the states of Sergipe and Alagoas and João Galafuz on Itamaracá.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Sergipe dan Alagoas ia juga disebut Jean de la foice atau Jean Delafosse (\"John si sabit\"), dan di Itamaracá João Galafuz.",
        "en": "In Sergipe and Alagoas it is also called Jean de la foice or Jean Delafosse (\"John of the sickle\"), and on Itamaracá João Galafuz."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c03",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "Alternatively, it is a mythical bull that shoots fire out of its nostrils.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam pengertian lain, ia banteng mitos yang menyemburkan api dari lubang hidungnya.",
        "en": "Alternatively it is a mythical bull that shoots fire from its nostrils."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c04",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "The term Boitatá is also used as papão ... the Portuguese and Brazilian bogeyman to frighten children into obedience.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah Boitatá juga dipakai sebagai papão, sosok momok untuk menakut-nakuti anak agar patuh.",
        "en": "The term Boitatá is also used as papão, the bogeyman used to frighten children into obedience."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c05",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "In a letter dated 31 May 1560, Father José de Anchieta described the apparition as the baetatá (meaning \"thing of fire\" or \"that which is entirely of fire\"), dwelling at most times near sea and rivers, encountered especially on beaches.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam surat bertanggal 31 Mei 1560, Pastor José de Anchieta menggambarkan penampakan baetatá (\"benda api\"), yang biasanya berada dekat laut dan sungai, terutama di pantai.",
        "en": "In a letter of 31 May 1560, Father José de Anchieta described the baetatá (\"thing of fire\"), usually found near sea and rivers, especially on beaches."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c06",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "What appears to be a glimmering beam of light coursing towards its victim will attack the Indians, burning and killing them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sesuatu yang tampak seperti berkas cahaya berkilau melesat ke arah korban dan menyerang, membakar, serta membunuh mereka.",
        "en": "Something like a glimmering beam of light rushes at its victims, burning and killing them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c07",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "The fire snake Boitatá (Mboitatá), according to Magalhães (1876), is one of the being subjected under the moon goddess Jaci ... the protectoress of all plants.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Magalhães (1876), ular api Boitatá adalah salah satu makhluk di bawah dewi bulan Jaci, pelindung semua tumbuhan.",
        "en": "According to Magalhães (1876), the fire snake Boitatá is one of the beings under the moon goddess Jaci, protector of all plants."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c08",
      "source_id": "boitata-s1",
      "quote": "The fire-snake sometimes transforms into a fiery log (called méuan) and burns the arsonist to death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ular api ini kadang menjelma kayu menyala (méuan) dan membakar pelaku pembakaran sampai mati.",
        "en": "The fire snake sometimes turns into a burning log (méuan) and burns the arsonist to death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c09",
      "source_id": "boitata-s2",
      "quote": "Esta criatura es una enorme serpiente de fuego que ataca a quien haga daño a los bosques y animales de la Amazonia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini ular api raksasa yang menyerang siapa pun yang merusak hutan dan satwa Amazon.",
        "en": "This creature is a huge fire serpent that attacks anyone who harms the forests and animals of the Amazon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "boitata-c10",
      "source_id": "boitata-s2",
      "quote": "parte del folklore de Brasil, conocido también en Paraguay, Uruguay y Argentina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Boitatá bagian dari cerita rakyat Brasil dan juga dikenal di Paraguay, Uruguay, dan Argentina.",
        "en": "It is part of Brazilian folklore and also known in Paraguay, Uruguay and Argentina."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Chonchon adalah kepala manusia bersayap dalam mitologi Mapuche, wujud yang diambil penyihir kalku, dan teriakannya \"tue tue tue\" dianggap pertanda kematian.",
    "en": "The Chonchon is a winged human head of Mapuche mythology, a form taken by kalku sorcerers, whose cry \"tue tue tue\" is taken as an omen of death.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "chonchon-c01",
      "chonchon-c04",
      "chonchon-c06",
      "chonchon-c08"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Chonchon adalah makhluk mitos dalam agama Mapuche serta mitologi rakyat Chili dan Argentina selatan. Ia berwujud kepala manusia berbulu dan bercakar yang terbang pada malam tanpa bulan dengan telinga besarnya sebagai sayap.",
      "en": "The Chonchon is a mythical creature of Mapuche religion and of the folk mythologies of Chile and southern Argentina. It is a feathered, taloned human head that flies on moonless nights using its huge ears as wings.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "chonchon-c01",
        "chonchon-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut mitos Mapuche, kepala terbang ini berasal dari orang, biasanya perempuan, yang berjanji dengan penyihir: pada malam hari kepalanya lepas dan terbang ke reni, tempat rahasia di bawah tanah tempat para penyihir berkumpul. Penyihir kalku sendiri juga bisa menjadi chonchon dengan mengoleskan krim ajaib di lehernya, dan dalam wujud ini ia dapat meminum darah orang sakit atau yang sedang tidur.",
      "en": "According to Mapuche myth the flying head comes from a person, usually a woman, who makes a pact with a sorcerer: at night her head detaches and flies to the reni, a secret underground gathering place of witches. A kalku sorcerer can also become a chonchon by anointing his throat with a magical cream, and in this form can drink the blood of the sick or sleeping.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "chonchon-c02",
        "chonchon-c03",
        "chonchon-c04",
        "chonchon-c05",
        "chonchon-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Orang biasa tidak dapat melihatnya, tetapi dapat mendengar teriakannya, \"tue tue tue\", yang dianggap pertanda kematian orang tercinta.",
      "en": "Ordinary people cannot see it but can hear its cry, \"tue tue tue\", taken as an omen of a loved one's death.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "chonchon-c08",
        "chonchon-c09"
      ]
    }

    {
      "id": "chonchon-c01",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "is a mythical creature found in Mapuche religion, as well as in the folk mythologies of Chile and southern Argentina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Chonchon adalah makhluk mitos dalam agama Mapuche serta mitologi rakyat Chili dan Argentina selatan.",
        "en": "The Chonchon is a mythical creature of Mapuche religion and the folk mythologies of Chile and southern Argentina."
      }

    {
      "id": "chonchon-c02",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "According to Mapuche myth the flying head is the result of someone, usually a woman, entering into a contract with a sorcerer or brujo; consequently her head detaches by night and sprout wings, so that the chonchón takes off in flight.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitos Mapuche, kepala terbang ini berasal dari seseorang, biasanya perempuan, yang membuat perjanjian dengan penyihir; kepalanya lepas pada malam hari, bersayap, lalu terbang.",
        "en": "According to Mapuche myth the flying head comes from someone, usually a woman, who makes a pact with a sorcerer; her head detaches at night, sprouts wings and flies off."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c03",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "There is supposedly a secret subterranean location called the reni, where she will fly to, to join her coven, to practice witchcraft or enjoy the festivities.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon ada tempat rahasia di bawah tanah bernama reni, tempat ia terbang untuk berkumpul dengan kelompok penyihirnya.",
        "en": "There is supposedly a secret underground place called the reni where she flies to join her coven."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c04",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "Thus the kalku wizard himself can transform into the flying head.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyihir kalku sendiri dapat berubah menjadi kepala terbang itu.",
        "en": "The kalku wizard himself can turn into the flying head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c05",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "To become a chonchon, the kalku undergoes a voluntary transformation facilitated by an anointing with a magical cream on the throat.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk menjadi chonchon, kalku mengoleskan krim ajaib di lehernya.",

    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c06",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "The chonchon takes the form of a human head adorned with feathers and talons. Its unusually large ears function as wings, enabling it to fly during moonless nights.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Chonchon berwujud kepala manusia berbulu dan bercakar; telinganya yang sangat besar berfungsi sebagai sayap untuk terbang pada malam tanpa bulan.",
        "en": "The chonchon is a human head with feathers and talons; its huge ears act as wings for flying on moonless nights."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c07",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "this transformation grants them additional abilities, including the ability to drink the blood of the sick or sleeping.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wujud ini memberi kemampuan tambahan, termasuk meminum darah orang sakit atau yang sedang tidur.",
        "en": "This form grants extra powers, including drinking the blood of the sick or sleeping."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c08",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s1",
      "quote": "they can still hear its distinctive cry of \"tue tue tue.\" This cry is considered a dire omen and often predicts the impending death of a loved one.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang biasa tetap dapat mendengar teriakannya, \"tue tue tue\", yang dianggap pertanda buruk akan kematian orang tercinta.",
        "en": "Ordinary people can still hear its cry, \"tue tue tue\", taken as a dire omen of a loved one's death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "chonchon-c09",
      "source_id": "chonchon-s2",
      "quote": "ホルヘ・ルイス・ボルヘス『幻獣辞典』の記述によると、妖術師にしかその姿を見せないとされており、「チュエ、チュエ」という声のみが聴こえるとされる。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Book of Imaginary Beings karya Jorge Luis Borges, chonchon hanya memperlihatkan diri kepada penyihir; orang lain hanya mendengar suaranya.",
        "en": "According to Jorge Luis Borges's Book of Imaginary Beings, the chonchon shows itself only to sorcerers; others only hear its cry."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    }
  ]
}

      "id": "Kurupi atau Curupi adalah tokoh mitologi Guaraní dan salah satu dari tujuh anak mengerikan Tau dan Kerana.       "en": "Kurupi or Curupi is a figure of Guaraní mythology and one of the seven monstrous children of Tau and Kerana.       "locator": null,

      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurupi adalah tokoh mitologi Guaraní yang dikenal karena alat kelaminnya yang sangat panjang hingga melilit pinggang, dan ditakuti sebagai penculik serta pemerkosa perempuan.",
        "en": "Kurupi is a figure of Guaraní mythology known for an elongated penis that winds around its waist, feared as an abductor and rapist of women."

    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c02",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "He is one of the seven monstrous children of Tau and Kerana, and as such is one of the central legendary figures in the region of Guaraní speaking cultures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia salah satu dari tujuh anak mengerikan Tau dan Kerana, sehingga menjadi tokoh legenda utama di wilayah berbahasa Guaraní.",
        "en": "He is one of the seven monstrous children of Tau and Kerana, and so a central legendary figure in Guaraní-speaking regions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c03",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "The modern so-called \"Paraguayan\" variety of Curupi/Kurupi is said to be short or small-bodied (like a child or be agéd), ugly, and hairy (or naked)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Versi modern yang disebut versi Paraguay menggambarkan Kurupi bertubuh pendek seperti anak atau orang tua, buruk rupa, dan berbulu.",
        "en": "The modern so-called Paraguayan Kurupi is short like a child or an old man, ugly and hairy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c04",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "This curupí's backward-pointing feet. will confuse anyone following its tracks and lead humans into the thicket or underbrush",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kakinya yang menghadap ke belakang mengecoh orang yang mengikuti jejaknya dan menyesatkan mereka ke semak belukar.",
        "en": "Its backward-pointing feet confuse anyone following its tracks and lead people into the thicket."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c05",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "It is also regarded as a protector of nature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dianggap pelindung alam.",
        "en": "It is also regarded as a protector of nature."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c06",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "According to some informants, it has the power of invisibility, and uses it as a form of defense, and kidnaps women and children using its penis as a lasso.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut sebagian narasumber, ia dapat menjadi tak terlihat untuk membela diri dan menculik perempuan serta anak-anak.",
        "en": "Some informants say it can turn invisible to defend itself and that it kidnaps women and children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c07",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s1",
      "quote": "The purported existence of the Curupi was logged in the 1793 diary of the explorer Juan Francisco Aguirre.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keberadaan Curupi tercatat dalam buku harian penjelajah Juan Francisco Aguirre tahun 1793.",
        "en": "The supposed existence of the Curupi was recorded in explorer Juan Francisco Aguirre's 1793 diary."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c08",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s2",
      "quote": "L'apparence de Kurupi est fréquemment rapprochée de celle de Pombero, un autre personnage important de la mythologie Guarani.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampilan Kurupi sering disamakan dengan Pombero, tokoh penting lain dalam mitologi Guaraní.",
        "en": "Kurupi's appearance is often compared to that of Pombero, another important figure of Guaraní mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c09",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s2",
      "quote": "Les Guaraní sont un groupe de populations amérindiennes des régions amazoniennes du Brésil et du Paraguay.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang Guaraní adalah kelompok penduduk asli Amerika dari wilayah Amazon di Brasil dan Paraguay.",
        "en": "The Guaraní are Indigenous American peoples of the Amazonian regions of Brazil and Paraguay."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "kurupi-c10",
      "source_id": "kurupi-s2",
      "quote": "Il est également l'une des rares figures légendaires encore prééminente dans les cultures actuelles de cette région.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kurupi termasuk sedikit tokoh legenda yang masih menonjol dalam budaya masa kini di wilayah itu.",
        "en": "Kurupi is one of the few legendary figures still prominent in the region's present-day cultures."

    "id": "Piasa adalah nama belakangan untuk makhluk pada mural penduduk asli Amerika di tebing Sungai Mississippi dekat Alton, Illinois, yang dicatat penjelajah Prancis pada 1673.",
    "en": "Piasa is a later name for the creature on a Native American cliff mural above the Mississippi near Alton, Illinois, recorded by French explorers in 1673.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "piasa-c01",

    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Piasa adalah nama yang diberikan belakangan untuk makhluk pada mural penduduk asli Amerika di tebing tinggi di atas Sungai Mississippi, di tempat yang kini menjadi Alton, Illinois. Pada 1673 penjelajah Prancis Marquette dan Louis Jolliet menggambarkannya sebagai makhluk berwarna-warni berwajah manusia dengan ekor panjang bersirip ikan. Sketsa sezaman menunjukkan makhluk tanpa sayap yang dapat dikenali sebagai jenis panter bawah air.",
      "en": "Piasa is a name given later to the creature on a Native American mural high on cliffs above the Mississippi, at what is now Alton, Illinois. In 1673 French explorers Marquette and Louis Jolliet described it as a multicoloured human-faced creature with a long fish-finned tail. A contemporary sketch shows a wingless beast identifiable as the underwater panther type.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "piasa-c01",
        "piasa-c02",
        "piasa-c03",
        "piasa-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Legenda kepala suku Ouatoga yang melawan burung Piasa dianggap para ahli folklor sebagai karangan John Russell (1836, 1848), dan satu sumber bahkan menyebut piasa sebagai makhluk rekaan abad ke-19. Dinding lukisan aslinya kemungkinan hancur sekitar tahun 1850-an, dan pada abad ke-20 mural itu beberapa kali dipulihkan dengan replika.",
      "en": "Folklorists regard the legend of chieftain Ouatoga fighting the Piasa bird as an invention of John Russell (1836, 1848), and one source even calls the piasa a 19th-century fabrication. The original painted wall was probably destroyed around the 1850s, and the mural was restored with replicas several times in the 20th century.",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-s2",
      "url": "https://es.wikipedia.org/wiki/Piasa",
      "title": "Piasa",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (es)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "es",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "piasa-c01",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "is a later name applied to the creature[s] from Native American mythology depicted in a mural painted by Native Americans high on cliffsides above the Mississippi River.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Piasa adalah nama belakangan untuk makhluk mitologi penduduk asli Amerika yang dilukis pada mural tinggi di tebing di atas Sungai Mississippi.",
        "en": "Piasa is a later name for the creature(s) of Native American mythology shown in a mural painted high on cliffs above the Mississippi River."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c02",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "Its original location was at the end of a chain of limestone bluffs in Madison County, Illinois, at present-day Alton, Illinois.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lokasi aslinya di ujung deretan tebing kapur di Madison County, kini Alton, Illinois.",
        "en": "Its original site was at the end of a chain of limestone bluffs in Madison County, at present-day Alton, Illinois."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c03",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "The original petrographs were described by French explorers Marquette and Louis Jolliet (1673) and described as a multicolored human-faced creature with a long tail terminating in fish-like fin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lukisan aslinya digambarkan penjelajah Prancis Marquette dan Louis Jolliet (1673) sebagai makhluk berwajah manusia, berwarna-warni, dengan ekor panjang bersirip ikan.",
        "en": "French explorers Marquette and Louis Jolliet (1673) described the original paintings as a multicoloured human-faced creature with a long fish-finned tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c04",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "Contemporary sketch of one of the beasts, which is wingless (and matching the description of a scaly quadruped with a man-like face and a long tail terminating in fishlike fins) is identifiable as the underwater panther type.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sketsa sezaman menunjukkan makhluk tanpa sayap, berkaki empat dan bersisik, yang dapat dikenali sebagai jenis panter bawah air.",
        "en": "A contemporary sketch shows a wingless, scaly four-legged beast identifiable as the underwater panther type."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c05",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "deeming the attached legend of the chieftain Ouatoga (who fended his tribe from the bird, protected by a divine invisible shield) to be fakelore, i.e., a piece of fiction by John Russell (1836, 1848)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para ahli folklor menganggap legenda kepala suku Ouatoga yang melawan burung itu sebagai cerita rakyat palsu karangan John Russell (1836, 1848).",
        "en": "Folklorists consider the legend of chieftain Ouatoga fighting the bird to be fakelore invented by John Russell (1836, 1848)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c06",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "The wall where the original paintings existed at Alton was probably destroyed ca. 1850s.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dinding tempat lukisan asli di Alton kemungkinan hancur sekitar tahun 1850-an.",
        "en": "The wall bearing the original paintings at Alton was probably destroyed around the 1850s."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c07",
      "source_id": "piasa-s1",
      "quote": "The mural has been restored with replicas a number of times in the 20th century",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada abad ke-20 mural ini beberapa kali dipulihkan dengan replika.",
        "en": "The mural was restored with replicas several times in the 20th century."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "piasa-c08",
      "source_id": "piasa-s2",
      "quote": "El piasa es una criatura ficticia inventada en el siglo XIX y hecha pasar por una supuesta criatura legendaria propia de la mitología de los nativos norteamericanos",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sumber lain menyebut piasa makhluk rekaan abad ke-19 yang diklaim sebagai makhluk legenda penduduk asli Amerika Utara.",
        "en": "Another source calls the piasa a 19th-century invention passed off as a legendary creature of Native North American mythology."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Keaslian tradisi Piasa",
        "en": "Authenticity of the Piasa tradition"
      },

      ]
    }
  ],

    "en": "Qʼuqʼumatz was the wind and rain god of the Kʼicheʼ Maya, the Feathered Serpent who, according to the Popol Vuh, created the world and humanity with Tepeu.",

    {
      "id": "Qʼuqʼumatz adalah dewa angin dan hujan bangsa Maya Kʼicheʼ zaman Pascaklasik. Menurut Popol Vuh, Ular Berbulu ini menciptakan dunia dan manusia bersama dewa Tepeu. Ia membawa matahari melintasi langit hingga ke dunia bawah dan menjadi penengah berbagai kekuatan dalam kosmos Maya. Namanya secara harfiah berarti \"ular quetzal\".",
      "en": "Qʼuqʼumatz was the wind and rain god of the Postclassic Kʼicheʼ Maya. According to the Popol Vuh, this Feathered Serpent created the world and humanity with the god Tepeu. It carried the sun across the sky into the underworld and mediated between the powers of the Maya cosmos. Its name literally means \"quetzal serpent\".",

      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",

      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",

      "statement": {

      "statement": {

    {
      "id": "q-uq-umatz-c05",
      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",
      "quote": "Together with Tepeu, god of lightning and fire, it was considered to be the mythical ancestor of the Kʼicheʼ nobility by direct male line.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",
      "quote": "Kotujaʼ, the Kʼicheʼ king who founded the city of Qʼumarkaj, bore the name of the deity as a title and was likely to have been a former priest of the god.",

      "context": "historical-record",

      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",
      "quote": "translates literally as \"quetzal serpent\" although it is often rendered less accurately as \"feathered serpent\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {

      "id": "q-uq-umatz-c08",
      "source_id": "q-uq-umatz-s1",
      "quote": "Qʼuqʼumatz may originally have been the same god as Tohil, the Kʼicheʼ sun god who also had attributes of the feathered serpent, but they later diverged and each deity came to have a separate priesthood.",

      "statement": {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Saci adalah tokoh populer mitologi Brasil: makhluk berkaki satu yang mengisap pipa dan memakai topi merah ajaib untuk muncul dan menghilang.",
    "en": "Saci is a popular figure of Brazilian mythology: a one-legged being who smokes a pipe and wears a magic red cap to appear and vanish.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "saci-folklore-c01",
      "saci-folklore-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Saci atau Saci-pererê adalah tokoh populer dalam mitologi Brasil: makhluk mirip kobold berkulit hitam dan berkaki satu yang mengisap pipa, meminta tembakau kepada orang, dan memakai topi merah ajaib untuk muncul dan menghilang, biasanya dalam pusaran debu. Tergantung daerahnya, ia bisa baik atau jahat; dalam versi jahatnya ia dipakai untuk menakut-nakuti anak di kalangan budak dan buruh tani.",
      "en": "The Saci or Saci-pererê is a popular figure of Brazilian mythology: a goblin-like, black-skinned, one-legged being who smokes a pipe, begs for tobacco and wears a magic red cap that lets him appear and vanish, usually in a dust devil. Depending on the region he may be good or bad; in his malicious version he served as a bogeyman for children among slaves and farm workers.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "saci-folklore-c01",
        "saci-folklore-c02",
        "saci-folklore-c03",
        "saci-folklore-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Saci yang tertangkap dapat dikurung dalam botol dan dipaksa mengabulkan permintaan sebagai tebusan kebebasannya. Namanya berasal dari bahasa Tupinambá: saci dari çua ci, \"mata jahat\", dan akhiran -pererê dari perérég, \"melompat-lompat\"; nama Saci-pererê awalnya merujuk pada burung kukuk bergaris.",
      "en": "A captured Saci can be shut in a bottle and forced to grant wishes in exchange for freedom. His name is Tupinambá: saci from çua ci, \"evil eye\", and the suffix -pererê from perérég, \"bouncy, jumpy\"; the name Saci-pererê originally referred to the striped cuckoo.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "saci-folklore-c05",
        "saci-folklore-c06",
        "saci-folklore-c07",
        "saci-folklore-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c01",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Der Saci oder Saci-pererê ist eine populäre Figur der brasilianischen Mythologie.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saci atau Saci-pererê adalah tokoh populer dalam mitologi Brasil.",
        "en": "The Saci or Saci-pererê is a popular figure of Brazilian mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c02",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Er ist ein koboldähnliches Wesen mit schwarzer Hautfarbe und nur einem Bein. Er raucht eine Pfeife, bettelt die Menschen um Tabak an und trägt eine magische rote Mütze, mit deren Hilfe er jederzeit auftauchen und wieder verschwinden kann (meist in einem Staubwirbel).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saci makhluk mirip kobold berkulit hitam dan berkaki satu yang mengisap pipa, meminta tembakau kepada orang, dan memakai topi merah ajaib untuk muncul dan menghilang kapan saja, biasanya dalam pusaran debu.",
        "en": "Saci is a goblin-like, black-skinned, one-legged being who smokes a pipe, begs people for tobacco and wears a magic red cap that lets him appear and vanish at any time, usually in a dust devil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c03",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "Je nach Region war er ein gutes oder schlechtes Wesen",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tergantung daerahnya, ia makhluk baik atau jahat.",
        "en": "Depending on the region he was a good or a bad being."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c04",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s2",
      "quote": "In seiner boshaften Varietät diente der Saci als Kinderschreck bei den Sklaven und Landarbeitern (Caboclos).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam versi jahatnya, Saci dipakai untuk menakut-nakuti anak di kalangan budak dan buruh tani (caboclo).",
        "en": "In his malicious version the Saci served as a bogeyman for children among slaves and farm workers (caboclos)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c05",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The captured Saci can be imprisoned inside a bottle, and be forced to grant wishes in exchange for freedom, just like Aladdin and the Magic Lamp in Arabian Nights.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saci yang tertangkap dapat dikurung dalam botol dan dipaksa mengabulkan permintaan sebagai tebusan kebebasannya, mirip kisah Aladin dan lampu ajaib.",
        "en": "A captured Saci can be shut in a bottle and made to grant wishes in exchange for freedom, like Aladdin and the Magic Lamp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c06",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The term saci derives from Tupinambá: çaci meaning \"sick eye\", or rather Tupinambá: çua ci \"evil eye\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata saci berasal dari bahasa Tupinambá çaci, \"mata sakit\", atau lebih tepatnya çua ci, \"mata jahat\".",
        "en": "The word saci comes from Tupinambá çaci, \"sick eye\", or rather çua ci, \"evil eye\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c07",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The suffixed -pererê also derives from Tupinambá perérég meaning \"bouncy, jumpy\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Akhiran -pererê berasal dari kata Tupinambá perérég yang berarti \"melompat-lompat\".",
        "en": "The suffix -pererê comes from Tupinambá perérég, meaning \"bouncy, jumpy\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "saci-folklore-c08",
      "source_id": "saci-folklore-s1",
      "quote": "The Saci-pererê of myth originally referred to a Cuculiformes (cuckoo family) bird, more specifically the striped cuckoo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Saci-pererê awalnya merujuk pada sejenis burung kukuk, yaitu kukuk bergaris.",
        "en": "The name Saci-pererê originally referred to a bird of the cuckoo family, the striped cuckoo."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Dalam agama Inuit, Adlivun adalah roh orang mati di dunia bawah, dan juga dunia bawah itu sendiri, tempat jiwa disucikan di bawah kekuasaan Sedna.",
    "en": "In Inuit religion, Adlivun are the spirits of the dead in the underworld, and the underworld itself, where souls are purified under Sedna's rule.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "adlivun-c01",
      "adlivun-c02",
      "adlivun-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam agama Inuit, Adlivun, yang berarti \"mereka yang hidup di bawah kita\", adalah roh orang mati yang tinggal di dunia bawah, dan juga nama dunia bawah itu sendiri, yang terletak di bawah daratan dan laut. Tempat ini biasanya digambarkan sebagai padang beku dan merupakan dunia pertama yang dituju orang mati.",
      "en": "In Inuit religion Adlivun, meaning \"those who live beneath us\", are the spirits of the dead living in the underworld, and also the name of that underworld beneath land and sea. It is usually described as a frozen wasteland and is the first world the dead go to.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "adlivun-c01",
        "adlivun-c03",
        "adlivun-c07",
        "adlivun-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pemandu jiwa Pinga dan Anguta membawa jiwa orang mati ke Adlivun, tempat mereka tinggal setahun dan disucikan sebelum berangkat ke Negeri Bulan. Sedna menguasai negeri itu dan menahan jiwa-jiwa sebagai persiapan tahap berikutnya. Jenazah seorang Inuk dibungkus kulit karibu lalu dikuburkan.",
      "en": "The psychopomps Pinga and Anguta bring the dead to Adlivun, where they stay a year and are purified before leaving for the Land of the Moon. Sedna rules the land and holds the souls to prepare them for the next stage. An Inuk's body is wrapped in caribou skin and buried.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "adlivun-c02",
        "adlivun-c04",
        "adlivun-c05",
        "adlivun-c06"
      ]
    }

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Alicanto adalah burung mitos dari Gurun Atacama, Chili, yang memakan bijih emas atau perak sehingga sayapnya bercahaya, dan dapat menuntun penambang ke harta.",
    "en": "The alicanto is a mythical bird of Chile's Atacama desert that eats gold or silver ore, making its wings glow, and can lead miners to treasure.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "alicanto-c01",
      "alicanto-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Chili, alicanto adalah burung malam dari Gurun Atacama yang tidak bisa terbang. Ia memakan bijih emas atau perak, sehingga sayapnya yang terbentang berkilau sesuai logam itu, dan bijih yang menumpuk di temboloknya membuatnya terlalu berat untuk terbang. Alicanto betina yang tinggal di gua bertelur hanya dua butir, dari emas atau perak.",
      "en": "In Chilean mythology the alicanto is a nocturnal bird of the Atacama desert that cannot fly. It eats gold or silver ore, so its outspread wings glitter with that metal, and the ore in its crop makes it too heavy to fly. The cave-dwelling female lays only two eggs, of gold or silver.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "alicanto-c01",
        "alicanto-c04",
        "alicanto-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Penambang yang mengikutinya tanpa ketahuan akan dituntun ke urat emas atau perak, tetapi bila burung itu menyadari kehadiran manusia, cahayanya padam dan ia bisa menuntun orang jatuh dari tebing. Cerita yang beredar menyebut penemuan perak tahun 1832 yang memicu demam perak Chili dilakukan Juan Godoy yang dituntun alicanto.",
      "en": "A miner who follows it unnoticed is led to a vein of gold or silver, but if the bird notices a human its light goes out and it may lead the person off a cliff. A popular story credits the 1832 silver find that set off Chile's silver rush to Juan Godoy, guided by an alicanto.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "alicanto-c02",
        "alicanto-c03",
        "alicanto-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Folkloris Julio Vicuña Cifuentes menguraikannya pada 1914/1915, dan alicanto juga dimuat dalam edisi Inggris Book of Imaginary Beings karya Borges.",
      "en": "Folklorist Julio Vicuña Cifuentes described it in 1914/1915, and the alicanto also appears in the English edition of Borges's Book of Imaginary Beings.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "alicanto-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c01",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s1",
      "quote": "The alicanto, in Chilean mythology, is a nocturnal flightless bird of the Atacama desert, which is said to run at night with glowing outspread wings, glittering in the gold or silver color deriving from the precious ores it supposedly eats.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Chili, alicanto adalah burung malam yang tidak bisa terbang dari Gurun Atacama; ia berlari dengan sayap terbentang yang berkilau emas atau perak dari bijih logam yang dimakannya.",
        "en": "In Chilean mythology the alicanto is a nocturnal flightless bird of the Atacama desert that runs at night with outspread wings glittering gold or silver from the ores it eats."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c02",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s1",
      "quote": "If the bird can be successfully detected without being noticed, it will lead the miner or prospector to a mother lode of gold or silver.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila diikuti tanpa ketahuan, burung ini menuntun penambang ke urat emas atau perak.",
        "en": "If followed unnoticed, the bird leads the miner to a rich vein of gold or silver."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c03",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s1",
      "quote": "But if the bird detects a human, it is said to extinguish its illumination, then disappear into darkness, or worse, lead the human to a perilous fall from a precipice.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila ia menyadari ada manusia, cahayanya padam dan ia menghilang dalam gelap, atau menuntun orang itu jatuh dari tebing.",
        "en": "If it notices a human, it puts out its light and vanishes into darkness, or leads the person off a cliff."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c04",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s1",
      "quote": "Also the cave-dwelling alicanto female will lay just two eggs, of gold or silver, depending on the ore it feeds on",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Alicanto betina yang tinggal di gua bertelur hanya dua butir, dari emas atau perak sesuai bijih yang dimakannya.",
        "en": "The cave-dwelling female lays only two eggs, of gold or silver depending on the ore it eats."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c05",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s1",
      "quote": "its flightlessness is due to the massive amount of ore it eats that sits in its crop and loads it down.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia tidak bisa terbang karena bijih logam yang dimakannya menumpuk di temboloknya dan memberatkannya.",
        "en": "It cannot fly because the ore it eats weighs it down in its crop."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c06",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s2",
      "quote": "チリのシルバーラッシュ ... の引き金になったのが、1832年5月16日の銀の露頭の発見であるが、これは鉱夫のフアン・ゴドイ ... がアリカント鳥に導かれて発見したのだという巷説がある。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita yang beredar, penemuan singkapan perak pada 16 Mei 1832 yang memicu demam perak Chili dilakukan penambang Juan Godoy yang dituntun burung alicanto.",
        "en": "A popular story holds that the silver outcrop found on 16 May 1832, which set off Chile's silver rush, was discovered by miner Juan Godoy guided by an alicanto."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alicanto-c07",
      "source_id": "alicanto-s2",
      "quote": "アリカントについては、民俗学者のフリオ・ヴィクーニャ・シフエンテス ... が1914/1915年に解説しているが、同様の内容が、ボルヘス著『幻獣辞典』 の拡張英訳版（1969年）の、「チリの動物 Fauna of Chile」の章に収められている。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Folkloris Julio Vicuña Cifuentes menguraikan alicanto pada 1914/1915, dan isi serupa dimuat di bab Fauna of Chile dalam edisi Inggris Book of Imaginary Beings karya Borges (1969).",
        "en": "Folklorist Julio Vicuña Cifuentes described the alicanto in 1914/1915, and similar content appears in the Fauna of Chile chapter of the expanded English edition of Borges's Book of Imaginary Beings (1969)."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Altamaha-ha adalah kriptid dalam cerita rakyat Georgia yang konon menghuni perairan dekat muara Sungai Altamaha.",
    "en": "The Altamaha-ha is a cryptid of Georgia folklore said to live in the waters near the mouth of the Altamaha River.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "altamaha-ha-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Georgia, Altamaha-ha atau Altie adalah makhluk legendaris yang konon menghuni anak-anak sungai dan bekas sawah dekat muara Sungai Altamaha. Penampakan terutama dilaporkan di sekitar Darien di McIntosh County. Menurut The Brunswick News legenda ini berakar pada tradisi Muscogee, dan satu sumber menyebut penduduk asli sudah percaya ada makhluk mirip ular raksasa di sungai itu sebelum penampakan resmi pertama tahun 1700.",
      "en": "The Altamaha-ha, also called Altie, belongs to the folklore of Georgia: it is said to haunt the streams and old rice fields where the Altamaha River meets the sea. Sightings are reported especially around Darien in McIntosh County. The Brunswick News traces the legend to Muscogee tradition, and one source says Native Americans already believed in a giant serpent-like creature in the river before the first formal sighting in 1700.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "altamaha-ha-c01",
        "altamaha-ha-c02",
        "altamaha-ha-c03",
        "altamaha-ha-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ikan alligator gar diajukan sebagai penjelasan penampakan belakangan ini. Pada 2018 bangkai di pantai Wolf Island sempat diduga Altamaha-ha, tetapi seniman Zardulu kemudian mengaku membuatnya dari hiu awetan dan bubur kertas. Makhluk ini juga muncul sebagai musuh dalam gim South of Midnight.",
      "en": "An alligator gar has been proposed to explain recent sightings. In 2018 remains on a Wolf Island beach were briefly thought to be an Altamaha-ha, but artist Zardulu later said he made them from a stuffed shark and papier-mâché. The creature also appears as a boss in the game South of Midnight.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "altamaha-ha-c04",
        "altamaha-ha-c05",
        "altamaha-ha-c06",

      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c01",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "In Georgia folklore, the Altamaha-ha (or Altie) is a legendary creature, alleged to inhabit the myriad small streams and abandoned rice fields near the mouth of the Altamaha River (after which it is named) in southeastern Georgia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Georgia, Altamaha-ha (Altie) adalah makhluk legendaris yang konon menghuni anak-anak sungai dan bekas sawah dekat muara Sungai Altamaha di Georgia tenggara.",
        "en": "In Georgia folklore the Altamaha-ha (Altie) is a legendary creature said to live in the streams and abandoned rice fields near the mouth of the Altamaha River in southeastern Georgia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c02",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "Sightings are particularly reported around Darien and elsewhere in McIntosh County.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampakan terutama dilaporkan di sekitar Darien dan tempat lain di McIntosh County.",
        "en": "Sightings are reported especially around Darien and elsewhere in McIntosh County."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c03",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "According to The Brunswick News, the legend has its roots in Muscogee tradition.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut The Brunswick News, legenda ini berakar pada tradisi Muscogee.",
        "en": "According to The Brunswick News, the legend is rooted in Muscogee tradition."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c04",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "An alligator gar has been proposed as being a possible identity for recent sightings attributed to the creature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ikan alligator gar diajukan sebagai kemungkinan penjelasan penampakan belakangan ini.",
        "en": "An alligator gar has been proposed as a possible explanation for recent sightings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c05",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "In 2018, decomposing remains were found on a beach in the Wolf Island National Wildlife Refuge, causing speculation that it may be the body of an Altamaha-ha.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2018 bangkai membusuk ditemukan di pantai Wolf Island National Wildlife Refuge dan diduga bangkai Altamaha-ha.",
        "en": "In 2018 decomposing remains found on a beach in the Wolf Island National Wildlife Refuge prompted speculation that they were an Altamaha-ha."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c06",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "Performance artist Zardulu later claimed responsibility for the remains, which were created out of a stuffed shark and papier-mâché.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seniman pertunjukan Zardulu kemudian mengaku membuatnya dari hiu yang diawetkan dan bubur kertas.",
        "en": "Performance artist Zardulu later claimed to have made the remains from a stuffed shark and papier-mâché."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c07",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s1",
      "quote": "In the videogame South of Midnight, Altamaha-ha is featured as a boss that Hazel encounters in a dark swamp.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam gim South of Midnight, Altamaha-ha muncul sebagai musuh utama yang ditemui Hazel di rawa gelap.",
        "en": "In the video game South of Midnight, Altamaha-ha is a boss Hazel meets in a dark swamp."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "altamaha-ha-c08",
      "source_id": "altamaha-ha-s2",
      "quote": "奥尔塔马霍河水怪的第一次正式目击可以追溯至1700年，更早之前的信息来自印第安人，他们认为奥尔塔马霍河生存着一只类似巨蛇的生物。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penampakan resmi pertama tercatat sejak 1700; informasi yang lebih tua berasal dari penduduk asli yang percaya ada makhluk mirip ular raksasa di Sungai Altamaha.",
        "en": "The first formal sighting dates to 1700; older information comes from Native Americans who believed a giant serpent-like creature lived in the Altamaha River."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Beast of Exmoor adalah kucing besar misterius yang dilaporkan berkeliaran di Exmoor, Devon dan Somerset, dan terkenal sejak pembantaian domba tahun 1983.",
    "en": "The Beast of Exmoor is a mysterious big cat reported to roam Exmoor in Devon and Somerset, famous since the sheep killings of 1983.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "beast-of-exmoor-c05",
      "beast-of-exmoor-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Beast of Exmoor adalah salah satu kisah kucing besar Britania yang paling terkenal: kucing kriptozoologi berbulu abu-abu gelap atau hitam yang dilaporkan berkeliaran di pedesaan Exmoor, terutama Devon dan Somerset. Penampakan dilaporkan sejak 1970, dan kisahnya menjadi berita nasional pada 1983 ketika lebih dari seratus domba mati dengan leher terkoyak di South Molton.",
      "en": "The Beast of Exmoor is one of the best-known British big cat stories: a cryptozoological cat with dark grey or black fur reported in rural Exmoor, especially Devon and Somerset. Sightings were reported from 1970, and the story became national news in 1983 when more than a hundred sheep were killed with torn throats at South Molton.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "beast-of-exmoor-c01",
        "beast-of-exmoor-c02",
        "beast-of-exmoor-c05",
        "beast-of-exmoor-c06",
        "beast-of-exmoor-c07"

    },
    {
      "id": "Makhluk itu diduga macan tutul hitam atau puma yang dilepas secara ilegal, tetapi tidak ada kucing besar yang pernah dipastikan, dan pembantaian 1983 kini dikaitkan dengan penyebab lain seperti anjing besar. Bangkai yang pernah diberitakan sebagai Beast of Exmoor ternyata anjing laut mati.",
      "en": "It was thought to be an illegally released black leopard or puma, but no big cat has ever been confirmed, and the 1983 killings are now attributed to causes such as large dogs. A carcass once reported as the Beast of Exmoor proved to be a dead seal.",

  ],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lusca adalah monster laut dalam cerita rakyat Karibia yang konon hidup di blue hole dekat Pulau Andros, Bahama, dan digambarkan sebagai gurita atau sotong raksasa.",
    "en": "The Lusca is a sea monster of Caribbean folklore said to live in the blue holes near Andros, Bahamas, described as a giant octopus or cuttlefish.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lusca-c01",
      "lusca-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Karibia, Lusca adalah monster laut yang konon hidup di blue hole dekat Andros, Bahama. Ia digambarkan sebagai gurita raksasa, sotong raksasa, atau setengah hiu setengah gurita; namanya penggalan dari Mollusca.",
      "en": "In Caribbean folklore the Lusca is a sea monster said to live in the blue holes near Andros in the Bahamas. It is described as a giant octopus, a giant cuttlefish or half shark, half octopus; its name is a clipped form of Mollusca.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lusca-c01",
        "lusca-c04",
        "lusca-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada 1872 J. S. George dari Nassau melaporkan gurita raksasa yang ditemukan mati di pantai. Kemudian pemandu setempat bercerita kepada ahli biologi laut Bruce S.       "en": "In 1872 J. S. George of Nassau reported a colossal octopus found dead on the beach. Later a local guide told marine biologist Bruce S.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lusca-c01",
      "source_id": "lusca-s1",
      "quote": "In Caribbean folklore, the Lusca is a name given to an alleged sea monster said to exist in the region of the blue holes nearby Andros, an island in The Bahamas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Karibia, Lusca adalah monster laut yang konon hidup di kawasan blue hole dekat Andros, sebuah pulau di Bahama.",
        "en": "In Caribbean folklore the Lusca is a sea monster said to live around the blue holes near Andros, an island in the Bahamas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lusca-c02",
      "source_id": "lusca-s1",
      "quote": "The first reported sighting of something resembling the traditional description of the Lusca in the area was in 1872, when J. S. George of Nassau in the Bahamas wrote to the American Naturalist describing a \"colossal octopus\" found dead on the beach.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laporan pertama yang mirip gambaran Lusca muncul pada 1872, ketika J. S. George dari Nassau menulis ke American Naturalist tentang \"gurita raksasa\" yang ditemukan mati di pantai.",
        "en": "The first report resembling the Lusca came in 1872, when J. S. George of Nassau wrote to the American Naturalist about a \"colossal octopus\" found dead on the beach."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lusca-c03",
      "source_id": "lusca-s1",
      "quote": "marine biologist Bruce S. Wright was told by a local guide that some limestone caverns beneath a lake were home to a monster called the lusca, described as a large, half-octopus, half-dragon animal, with powerful, sucker-tipped tentacles, which lived in banana holes and blue holes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahli biologi laut Bruce S. Wright mendengar dari pemandu setempat bahwa gua kapur di bawah sebuah danau dihuni lusca, makhluk besar setengah gurita setengah naga dengan tentakel bermangkuk isap.",
        "en": "Marine biologist Bruce S. Wright was told by a local guide that limestone caves under a lake were home to the lusca, a large half-octopus, half-dragon animal with sucker-tipped tentacles."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lusca-c04",
      "source_id": "lusca-s1",
      "quote": "Lusca is a clipped form of Mollusca (a phylum containing octopus, squid, cuttlefish, and other cephalopods), referring to the creature's varied mollusk features, such as tentacles.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama Lusca adalah penggalan dari Mollusca, merujuk pada ciri moluska makhluk ini seperti tentakel.",
        "en": "Lusca is a clipped form of Mollusca, referring to the creature's mollusc features such as tentacles."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lusca-c05",
      "source_id": "lusca-s1",
      "quote": "It is described as a giant octopus, a giant cuttlefish, or a half shark, half octopus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai gurita raksasa, sotong raksasa, atau setengah hiu setengah gurita.",
        "en": "It is described as a giant octopus, a giant cuttlefish, or half shark, half octopus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lusca-c06",
      "source_id": "lusca-s2",
      "quote": "Le monstre de St. Augustin qui a été trouvé sur les côtes de Floride en 1896 est l'un des meilleurs candidats pour un possible spécimen de lusca.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster St. Augustine yang terdampar di pantai Florida pada 1896 dianggap salah satu kandidat terkuat spesimen lusca.",
        "en": "The St. Augustine monster found on the Florida coast in 1896 is considered one of the best candidates for a lusca specimen."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Minhocão adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Brasil, digambarkan sebagai cacing atau ikan raksasa yang menyeret ternak ke dalam air dan meninggalkan parit dalam.",
    "en": "The Minhocão is a creature of Brazilian folklore, described as a giant worm or fish that drags livestock under water and leaves deep trenches.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "minhocao-legendary-creature-c01",
      "minhocao-legendary-creature-c03",
      "minhocao-legendary-creature-c07"
    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Minhocão adalah makhluk cerita rakyat Brasil yang diklaim berupa ikan besar atau menyerupai cacing raksasa; namanya bentuk pembesar dari minhoca, \"cacing tanah\" dalam bahasa Portugis. Ia konon mencabut pohon, meninggalkan parit dalam, dan menyukai tempat lembap.",
      "en": "The Minhocão is a creature of Brazilian folklore said to be a large fish or a giant worm; its name is an augmentative of minhoca, Portuguese for \"earthworm\". It was said to uproot trees, leave deep trenches and prefer damp places.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "minhocao-legendary-creature-c01",
        "minhocao-legendary-creature-c04",
        "minhocao-legendary-creature-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Penduduk setempat menceritakannya kepada naturalis Eropa pada abad ke-19. Pada Desember 1846 Augustin Saint-Hilaire mencatat Minhocão di danau Padre Aranda dan Feia yang konon menyeret kuda dan sapi ke dalam air, dan menduganya sejenis ikan paru besar. Fritz Müller melaporkannya dalam jurnal Jerman pada 1878, dan kriptozoolog Karl Shuker kemudian mengaitkannya dengan Sachamama dari Peru.",
      "en": "Locals described it to European naturalists in the 19th century. In December 1846 Augustin Saint-Hilaire recorded Minhocão in the Padre Aranda and Feia lakes that dragged horses and cattle under water, and guessed it was a large lungfish.   "short_description": {
    "id": "Patagon adalah ras raksasa mitos yang menurut catatan Eropa awal tinggal di Patagonia, pertama kali dilaporkan oleh pelayaran Magellan pada tahun 1520-an.",
    "en": "The Patagons were a mythical race of giants that early European accounts placed in Patagonia, first reported by Magellan's voyage in the 1520s.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "patagons-c01",
      "patagons-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Patagon atau raksasa Patagonia adalah ras manusia raksasa yang menurut catatan Eropa awal tinggal di Patagonia. Mereka disebut setidaknya dua kali tinggi manusia biasa, bahkan 13 hingga 15 kaki menurut beberapa catatan. Nama Patagonia sendiri berasal dari arti \"tanah tempat tinggal Patagon\".",
      "en": "The Patagons or Patagonian giants were a race of giant humans that early European accounts placed in Patagonia. They were said to be at least twice normal height, some accounts saying 13 to 15 feet. The name Patagonia itself comes from \"land where the Patagons live\".",
      "claim_ids": [
        "patagons-c01",
        "patagons-c02",
        "patagons-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Penyebutan pertama berasal dari pelayaran keliling dunia Ferdinand Magellan pada tahun 1520-an. Penulis catatannya, Pigafetta, menggambarkan lelaki raksasa yang menari dan bernyanyi di pantai, begitu tinggi sehingga para pelaut hanya setinggi pinggangnya, dan mencatat bahwa Magellan menamai mereka Patagão. Kisah ini memengaruhi gambaran Eropa tentang wilayah itu selama berabad-abad hingga dibantah pada akhir abad ke-18.",
      "en": "The first mention came from Ferdinand Magellan's circumnavigation in the 1520s. Its chronicler Pigafetta described a giant dancing and singing on the shore, so tall the sailors reached only his waist, and recorded that Magellan named them Patagão. The story shaped European ideas of the region for centuries until it was disproved in the late 18th century.",

      "title": "パタゴン",
      "author": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "patagons-c01",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "The Patagons or Patagonian giants were a mythical race of giant humans rumoured to be living in Patagonia described in early European accounts.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Patagon atau raksasa Patagonia adalah ras manusia raksasa mitos yang konon tinggal di Patagonia menurut catatan Eropa awal.",
        "en": "The Patagons or Patagonian giants were a mythical race of giant humans said in early European accounts to live in Patagonia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c02",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "They were said to have exceeded at least double normal human height, with some accounts giving heights of 13 to 15 feet",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka disebut setidaknya dua kali tinggi manusia biasa, dengan beberapa catatan menyebut 13 hingga 15 kaki.",
        "en": "They were said to be at least twice normal human height, some accounts giving 13 to 15 feet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c03",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "Tales of these people maintained a hold upon European conceptions of the region for nearly 300 years.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kisah mereka memengaruhi gambaran Eropa tentang wilayah itu selama hampir 300 tahun.",
        "en": "Tales of them shaped European ideas of the region for nearly 300 years."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c04",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "The first mention of these people came from the voyage of Ferdinand Magellan and his crew, who claimed to have seen them while exploring the coastline of South America en route to the Maluku Islands in their circumnavigation of the world in the 1520s.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyebutan pertama berasal dari pelayaran Ferdinand Magellan dan awaknya, yang mengaku melihat mereka saat menyusuri pantai Amerika Selatan dalam pelayaran keliling dunia tahun 1520-an.",
        "en": "The first mention came from Ferdinand Magellan and his crew, who claimed to see them while sailing the South American coast during their 1520s circumnavigation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c05",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "One day we suddenly saw a naked man of giant stature on the shore of the port, dancing, singing, and throwing dust on his head.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Catatan pelayaran itu menyebut seorang lelaki telanjang bertubuh raksasa terlihat di pantai, menari, bernyanyi, dan menaburkan debu di kepalanya.",
        "en": "The voyage account says a naked man of giant stature was seen on the shore, dancing, singing and throwing dust on his head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c06",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "He was so tall that we reached only to his waist, and he was well proportioned",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia begitu tinggi sehingga para pelaut hanya setinggi pinggangnya.",
        "en": "He was so tall that the sailors reached only to his waist."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c07",
      "source_id": "patagons-s1",
      "quote": "Pigafetta also recorded that Magellan had bestowed on these people the name \"Patagão\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pigafetta mencatat bahwa Magellan memberi mereka nama Patagão.",
        "en": "Pigafetta recorded that Magellan gave these people the name Patagão."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c08",
      "source_id": "patagons-s2",
      "quote": "Cerita mengenai Patagon ini terus dipercaya selama 250 tahun, hingga dibuktikan kebohongannya pada akhir abad ke-18.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cerita tentang Patagon dipercaya selama 250 tahun hingga dibuktikan tidak benar pada akhir abad ke-18.",
        "en": "The Patagon story was believed for 250 years until it was disproved in the late 18th century."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "patagons-c09",
      "source_id": "patagons-s3",
      "quote": "現在の南米パタゴニアの地名は「パタゴンの住む土地」の意からきている。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama wilayah Patagonia berasal dari arti \"tanah tempat tinggal Patagon\".",
        "en": "The place name Patagonia comes from the meaning \"land where the Patagons live\"."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Lama kisah dipercaya",
        "en": "How long the story was believed"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Hampir 300 tahun.",
            "en": "Nearly 300 years."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Pope Lick Monster adalah makhluk legenda urban setengah manusia setengah kambing yang konon tinggal di bawah jembatan kereta di Louisville, Kentucky.",
    "en": "The Pope Lick Monster is an urban-legend part-man, part-goat creature said to live under a railroad trestle in Louisville, Kentucky.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "pope-lick-monster-c01"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pope Lick Monster, sering dijuluki Goat Man, adalah makhluk legendaris setengah manusia, setengah kambing dan domba yang konon hidup di bawah jembatan kereta di atas Pope Lick Creek di Louisville, Kentucky. Menurut sebagian kisah, ia memikat orang ke jembatan dengan hipnosis atau peniruan suara hingga tewas tertabrak kereta; kisah lain menyebut ia melompat ke atap mobil yang lewat.",
      "en": "The Pope Lick Monster, often called the Goat Man, is a legendary part-man, part-goat, part-sheep creature said to live under a railroad trestle over Pope Lick Creek in Louisville, Kentucky. Some accounts say it lures people onto the trestle with hypnosis or voice mimicry to be killed by trains; others say it leaps onto passing cars.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pope-lick-monster-c01",
        "pope-lick-monster-c02",
        "pope-lick-monster-c03",
        "pope-lick-monster-c07"

    },
    {
      "id": "Satu legenda menyebut ia dulunya penampil sirkus yang membalas dendam setelah dianiaya. Legenda ini menarik pemburu sensasi, dan sudah terjadi sejumlah kematian serta kecelakaan di jembatan yang sebenarnya masih menjadi jalur kereta utama itu.",
      "en": "One legend says it was a mistreated circus performer seeking revenge. The legend draws thrill-seekers, and there have been several deaths and accidents on the trestle, which still carries a major rail line.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "pope-lick-monster-c04",
